{"forms":[{"form":"nemmine","tags":["alternative","pronunciation-spelling"]},{"form":"nvm","tags":["alternative","abbreviation"]},{"form":"nm","tags":["alternative","abbreviation"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"I'm afraid I’ve broken your mug. — Never mind, it was old and I was going to throw it away.","type":"example"},{"text":"Did you fall over and hurt your knee? Never mind, I’ll put a bandage on it.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["It is not important; do not fret; used to reassure or comfort the person to whom it is said."],"tags":["hortative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Never mind about me — you go and I’ll join you later.","type":"example"},{"text":"Here’s some money for you. Never mind about paying me back; you can keep it.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Do not be concerned (about someone or something, or about doing something)."],"tags":["hortative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"You're a fool. — What did you call me? — Never mind.","type":"example"},{"text":"Can you hand me that screwdriver? Wait, never mind, give me the fire extinguisher instead.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Indicates a withdrawal or retractment of a previous statement."],"tags":["hortative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"They wouldn't go near that place, never mind spending an entire day there.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Let alone; much less."],"tags":["idiomatic"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈnɛ.vɚˌmaɪnd/","tags":["US"]},{"ipa":"/ˈnɛv.ə(ɹ)ˌmaɪnd/","tags":["UK"]}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"It is not important","word":"forget about it"},{"sense":"It is not important","word":"forget it"},{"sense":"Do not be concerned","word":"pay someone no mind"},{"sense":"Withdrawal of previous statement","word":"belay my last"}],"upstream_line":254237,"upstream_offset":1262509377,"upstream_record_sha256":"f04d3177f7487052aa4df4f14f19f74f949fdeb45c618685e9679fe19eea6b6a","word":"never mind"}
